The [Magic Online Championship Series](/en/articles/feature/2009/05-07-magic-online-championship-series) awards invites to the [Magic World Championships](https://web.archive.org/web/20090921000000/https://magic.wizards.com/en/node/642286) as well as a cash tournament for players who qualify through the series. The 2009 Season 6 Championship was held on September 20. The format was *Masters Edition III* sealed deck with a Top 8 *Masters Edition III* draft. Click below to launch the draft viewer to see how they drafted.

[](https://web.archive.org/web/20090921000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mocs2009s6)

### [Launch the Draft Viewer](https://web.archive.org/web/20090921000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mocs2009s6)

Here's how the top 8 matches occurred.

## top 8 bracket

### Quaterfinals

**CharToYourFace** 

PAWNLERO 

**JustMeBaby** 

BaronVonChickenPants 

**Anthony Sansone** 

The Ervin Tormos Jazz Hour 

**nodnol** 

about2punt 

### Semi-finals

**CharToYourFace, 2-1** 

JustMeBaby, 2-0 

**Anthony Sansone, 2-0** 

nodnol, 2-1 

### Finals

**CharToYourFace, 2-1** 

Anthony Sansone, 2-1 

### Champion

**CharToYourFace, 2-0**
